# Top-500 Hausa word review queue

Each completed row is a permanent corpus-wide lexical decision: every occurrence of that Boko word benefits from the same review. `VOWEL_LENGTH` asks whether a written Boko vowel is short or long; Boko spelling does not encode that distinction, while fully vowelled Ajami must.

Leaving a decision blank is safe. The row remains `provisional` or `blocked`, is not approved, and is not shipped. A blocked Ajami cell means the existing rules cannot produce a complete form without guessing.

Corpus: 259,490 running word tokens and 3,449 lowercase-folded forms. Structural markers and template placeholders are removed before counting; non-Hausa-orthography tokens are excluded from ranking and recorded separately. This selected list contains 434 forms and covers 3.3820% of running text.

Ordering: entries are sorted by their immutable corpus frequency rank.
